Next step decision
P4.10.0-C8-I-D4-L-L-O-BV - Controlled Activity Intake First Real Auth Session Runtime Read Browser Verification Contract Verification

Contract layer name
controlled-activity-intake-first-real-auth-session-browser-verification-contract-v1

Reason for O-BU
O-BT approved only a browser verification contract step.
O-BU defines the exact browser verification contract for a future no-write real Auth0/session runtime-read test.
O-BU does not modify the diagnostic endpoint.
O-BU does not execute a real Auth0/session runtime read.
O-BU does not read Supabase.
O-BU does not call AI.

Current verified state
- Diagnostic endpoint exists:
  src/app/api/activity/debug/real-auth-session-runtime-read-test/route.ts
- Diagnostic endpoint remains static/fail-closed.
- FIRST_REAL_AUTH_SESSION_RUNTIME_READ_APPROVED=false remains in the endpoint.
- Diagnostic endpoint does not import Auth0 SDK.
- Diagnostic endpoint does not call getSession/getAccessToken.
- Diagnostic endpoint does not call cookies()/headers().
- Diagnostic endpoint does not call bridge yet.
- Controlled-intake route.ts remains unchanged.
- productionWriteEnabled=false remains in controlled-intake route.ts.
- No Supabase read/write is approved.
- No activity_events insert is approved.
- No AI call is approved.

Future browser verification endpoint
- URL: /api/activity/debug/real-auth-session-runtime-read-test
- Method: GET only
- Required header: x-controlled-intake-first-real-session-read-test: true
- Request credentials: include
- Boundary: debug endpoint only
- Purpose: prove browser-authenticated request can produce a safe Auth0 subject without DB/SQL/AI/write

Future implementation prerequisite
Before the browser verification can run, a later implementation gate must explicitly enable the real session read in the diagnostic endpoint.
That future implementation must remain restricted to the diagnostic endpoint and its report.
It must not modify controlled-intake route.ts.
It must not read Supabase.
It must not write activity_events.

Required future Auth0/session helper contract
The future implementation must identify and use exactly one approved project Auth0/session server helper.
The helper must be called only after:
- diagnostic header is present and exactly true after trim/lowercase;
- internal implementation approval constant is true;
- endpoint is the diagnostic endpoint;
- no request body is read;
- no client ownership fields are accepted.

Required session extraction contract
The future implementation must extract only:
- provider, normalized to auth0;
- stable authSubject from Auth0 sub/authSubject;
- email if present and safe;
- sessionId if present and safe;
- issuedAt/expiresAt if present and safe.

The future implementation must fail if:
- session is missing;
- sub/authSubject is missing;
- sub/authSubject is blank;
- identity is email-only;
- provider is unsupported;
- session appears expired;
- helper throws;
- helper returns unsafe payload.

Required sanitization contract
Before passing anything into bridge or response:
- remove tokens;
- remove raw cookies;
- remove raw session objects;
- remove raw profile objects;
- remove full provider payloads;
- remove roles/permissions;
- remove app/user metadata;
- keep only the sanitized fields needed by the bridge.

Required bridge contract
After sanitization, the diagnostic endpoint should pass sanitized session data to:
- buildControlledActivityIntakeServerSideAuthSessionRuntimeBridge

The bridge path must remain:
- serverSideAuthSessionRuntimeBridge.ts
- serverSideAuthSessionRuntimeConnection.ts
- serverSideAuthSessionRuntime.ts
- serverSideAuthSession.ts

Required success HTTP contract
Authenticated browser + valid header + safe session:
- HTTP 200
- ok=true
- stage="first_real_auth_session_runtime_read_test"
- layer="controlled-activity-intake-first-real-auth-session-runtime-read-test-v1"
- requestSource
- sessionPresent=true
- provider="auth0"
- authSubject present and non-empty
- email only if present and safe
- identity.provider="auth0"
- identity.authSubject present and non-empty
- connectionBridgeStatus.connectionOk=true
- connectionBridgeStatus.runtimeOk=true
- connectionBridgeStatus.staticIdentityOk=true
- guardrails.debugEndpointOnly=true
- guardrails.diagnosticReadOnly=true
- guardrails.controlledIntakeRouteChanged=false
- guardrails.realAuth0SessionReadAllowed=true
- guardrails.realAuth0SessionReadExecuted=true
- guardrails.auth0RuntimeReadExecuted=true
- guardrails.serverSideSessionReadOnly=true
- guardrails.clientOwnershipAccepted=false
- guardrails.previewHeaderAcceptedForProductionWrite=false
- guardrails.dbReadExecuted=false
- guardrails.dbWriteExecuted=false
- guardrails.sqlExecuted=false
- guardrails.aiCallExecuted=false
- guardrails.activityEventsInserted=false
- guardrails.semanticCandidatesPersisted=false
- guardrails.valueObjectsCreated=false
- guardrails.stateFactsCreated=false
- guardrails.stateDeltasCreated=false
- guardrails.stateSnapshotsCreated=false

Required unauthenticated HTTP contract
Unauthenticated browser + valid header:
- HTTP 401
- ok=false
- code=CONTROLLED_INTAKE_SERVER_SESSION_REQUIRED
- sessionPresent=false
- no tokens
- no raw session
- no DB/SQL/AI/write activity

Required missing-header contract
Missing or wrong diagnostic header:
- HTTP 403
- ok=false
- code=CONTROLLED_INTAKE_FIRST_REAL_SESSION_DEBUG_HEADER_REQUIRED
- no Auth0/session helper call
- guardrails.realAuth0SessionReadAllowed=false or not applicable
- guardrails.realAuth0SessionReadExecuted=false
- guardrails.auth0RuntimeReadExecuted=false
- guardrails.dbReadExecuted=false
- guardrails.dbWriteExecuted=false
- guardrails.sqlExecuted=false
- guardrails.aiCallExecuted=false
- guardrails.activityEventsInserted=false

Required unsafe-payload contract
Unsafe helper payload:
- HTTP 500 or 403 depending on failure location
- ok=false
- code=CONTROLLED_INTAKE_FIRST_REAL_SESSION_UNSAFE_PAYLOAD or bridge unsafe failure code
- sanitized message only
- no raw exception
- no raw session
- no tokens
- no provider payload

Required failure codes for future browser verification
- CONTROLLED_INTAKE_FIRST_REAL_SESSION_READ_NOT_APPROVED
- CONTROLLED_INTAKE_FIRST_REAL_SESSION_DEBUG_HEADER_REQUIRED
- CONTROLLED_INTAKE_FIRST_REAL_SESSION_BOUNDARY_UNAVAILABLE
- CONTROLLED_INTAKE_FIRST_REAL_SESSION_READ_FAILED
- CONTROLLED_INTAKE_FIRST_REAL_SESSION_UNSAFE_PAYLOAD
- CONTROLLED_INTAKE_SERVER_SESSION_REQUIRED
- CONTROLLED_INTAKE_AUTH_SUBJECT_REQUIRED
- CONTROLLED_INTAKE_AUTH_SESSION_EXPIRED
- CONTROLLED_INTAKE_AUTH_SESSION_INVALID
- CONTROLLED_INTAKE_AUTH_PROVIDER_UNSUPPORTED
- CONTROLLED_INTAKE_AUTH_BRIDGE_RUNTIME_FAILED
- CONTROLLED_INTAKE_AUTH_BRIDGE_IDENTITY_FAILED
- CONTROLLED_INTAKE_AUTH_BRIDGE_UNSAFE_RESULT

Forbidden response fields
The future browser verification endpoint must never return:
- access_token
- refresh_token
- id_token
- token
- raw cookie
- raw session
- raw profile
- full Auth0 user object
- full provider payload
- roles
- permissions
- app metadata
- user metadata
- appUserId
- user_id
- actorId
- organizationId
- spaceId
- membership rows
- Supabase rows
- activity_events rows
- semantic candidates
- value objects
- state facts/deltas/snapshots

Required future browser console verification script behavior
The browser console script must:
1. Call /api/activity/debug/real-auth-session-runtime-read-test without header and expect HTTP 403.
2. Call /api/activity/debug/real-auth-session-runtime-read-test with wrong header and expect HTTP 403.
3. Call /api/activity/debug/real-auth-session-runtime-read-test with x-controlled-intake-first-real-session-read-test:true and credentials included.
4. Accept either:
   - authenticated success: HTTP 200 with sanitized Auth0 subject; or
   - unauthenticated controlled failure: HTTP 401 with CONTROLLED_INTAKE_SERVER_SESSION_REQUIRED.
5. Fail if any forbidden field appears in raw JSON text.
6. Fail if dbReadExecuted/dbWriteExecuted/sqlExecuted/aiCallExecuted/activityEventsInserted are not false.
7. Fail if controlledIntakeRouteChanged is not false.
8. Print HTTP status, content-type, parsed JSON, selected guardrail checks, forbidden-field checks, and final PASS/FAIL line.

Required future browser console script forbidden-field list
- access_token
- refresh_token
- id_token
- rawSession
- rawProfile
- raw cookie
- raw session
- raw profile
- full Auth0 user object
- full provider payload
- appUserId
- user_id
- actorId
- organizationId
- spaceId
- memberships
- activity_events
- semanticCandidates
- valueObjects
- stateFacts
- stateDeltas
- stateSnapshots

Required future static verification before browser run
The implementation verification must prove:
- diagnostic endpoint path exists.
- controlled-intake route.ts unchanged.
- existing helpers unchanged.
- npx tsc --noEmit passes.
- no Supabase client import unless explicitly approved later.
- no createClient.
- no .from(.
- no .insert(.
- no .update(.
- no .delete(.
- no .rpc(.
- no SQL text.
- no OpenAI import.
- no chat.completions.
- no activity_events insert.
- no category/value/state persistence.
- no forbidden response-field strings are returned in success or failure payloads.

Required future route immutability proof
The implementation and browser verification must show:
- src/app/api/activity/controlled-intake/route.ts has no diff.
- productionWriteEnabled=false remains present.
- no runtime adapter integration into controlled-intake route.ts.
- no production write path.

Required future no-write proof in browser response
Every browser response must include guardrails proving:
- debugEndpointOnly=true
- diagnosticReadOnly=true
- controlledIntakeRouteChanged=false
- serverSideSessionReadOnly=true
- clientOwnershipAccepted=false
- previewHeaderAcceptedForProductionWrite=false
- dbReadExecuted=false
- dbWriteExecuted=false
- sqlExecuted=false
- aiCallExecuted=false
- activityEventsInserted=false
- semanticCandidatesPersisted=false
- valueObjectsCreated=false
- stateFactsCreated=false
- stateDeltasCreated=false
- stateSnapshotsCreated=false

Important separation
This browser verification is not app-user mapping.
This browser verification is not actor/org/space resolution.
This browser verification is not organization membership resolution.
This browser verification is not production trust for writes.
This browser verification only proves that a browser-authenticated request can produce a safe Auth0 subject through a no-write diagnostic boundary.

Local evidence collection note
O-BU collected local Auth0/session/browser verification evidence with git grep only.
This is not an Auth0 runtime call.
This is not a session read.
This is not a Supabase read.
This is not SQL execution.
This is not a DB write.
